   SELECT FUN_GET_COMPANY_NAME(INT_COMPANYID),FUN_GET_BRANCH_NAME(INT_BRANCHID),   CHR_EMPID,CHR_STAFFNAME,CHR_STAFFFNAME,   FUN_GET_DEPARTMENT_NAME(INT_DEPARTID), FUN_GET_DESIGNATION_NAME(INT_DESIGID),   FUN_GET_OFFICE_NAME(INT_OFFICEID), CHR_GENDER,DATE_FORMAT(DT_DOB,'%d-%m-%Y'),   CHR_BG, DATE_FORMAT(DT_DOJCOLLEGE,'%d-%m-%Y'),  INT_BALANCE,CHR_TYPE,   INT_SALARY,CHR_PF,CHR_ESI,INT_GROSSSALARY,CHR_MARITALSTATUS,    DATE_FORMAT(DT_WEDDATE,'%d-%m-%Y'), CHR_BANK,CHR_ACCNO,   CHR_PADD1,CHR_PADD2,CHR_PCITY,CHR_PSTATE,CHR_PPIN,CHR_SADD1,CHR_SADD2,   CHR_SCITY, CHR_SSTATE,CHR_SPIN,CHR_PPHONE,CHR_SPHONE,CHR_EMAILID,CHR_MOBILE,   CHR_PFTYPE,INT_PFAMT, CHR_PFNO,CHR_ESITYPE,INT_ESIAMT,CHR_ESINO,CHR_LEAVECARRY,   CHR_QUALIFICATION,CHR_MANAGEMENT, FIND_A_EMPLOYEE_ID_NAME(CHR_REPTO),CHR_REP,CHR_HOLD,INT_PLACEID,   CHR_CONVEYANCECLEAR,CHR_SALARYCALCULATE,CHR_PAYSLIPHIDE, CHR_CATEGORY,CHR_USERID,    DATE_FORMAT(DAT_PFESIDATE,'%d-%m-%Y'),DATE_FORMAT(DAT_RESIGNDATE,'%d-%m-%Y'),    CHR_PAN,CHR_PASSPORT,CHR_DRIVING, CHR_PERMAILID,CHR_PREVIOUSDETAILS,   CHR_MOTHERNAME,CHR_COMPANYPFNO,CHR_ADDRESSSTATUS, CHR_FUTURE10,INT_PDISTRICTID,   INT_PCOUNTRYID,INT_CDISTRICTID,INT_CCOUNTRY,CHR_ADDRESSPROOFSTATUS, CHR_PFCLAIMSTATUS,   DATE_FORMAT(DAT_PFCLIAMDATE,'%d-%m-%Y'),CHR_PFCLIAMDESCRIPTION,    DATE_FORMAT(DAT_PFRESIGNDATE,'%d-%m-%Y'),CHR_INTERVIEW,    CHR_REJOIN,CHR_NOMINEE,CHR_SALARYSPLITUP,DATE_FORMAT(DT_CREATION_DATE,'%d-%m-%Y'),   CHR_CREATEDBY, DATE_FORMAT(DAT_RIVISION,'%d-%m-%Y'), INT_CTC,INT_GROSS_PERCENT,   INT_VARIABLE_PERCENT,CHR_IFSC,INT_ATTENDANCECYCLE,CHR_SALARYTYPE,   FUN_GET_LOCATION_NAME(INT_LOCATIONID),CHR_NOMINEE_RELATIONSHIP,CHR_SPOUSENAME ,   DATE_FORMAT(DT_ACTUAL_DOB,'%d-%m-%Y'), CHR_MODIFICATION,CHR_APPROVED_DESC,CONCAT('Type-',CHR_PFCALCULATIONTYPE),CHR_AADHARNO,CHR_PF_UAN, CHR_REFEMPID,INT_CONVEYANCE_LIMIT  FROM com_m_staff WHERE CHR_EMPID='SEP2022MSL100038'

 (SELECT  a.CHR_VENDORPO InvNumber, DATE_FORMAT(a.DAT_ORDERDATE,'%d-%b-%Y') InvDate, e.CHR_VENDORNAME NameoftheVendor,   e.CHR_GSTNO  VendorGSTNo,   j.CHR_MODELCODE Brand ,  f.CHR_GROUPNAME ProductGroup, c.CHR_DES PCODEDESCRIPTION ,     FUN_INV_GET_WARRANTY_IN_YEAR(b.INT_WARRANTY) Warranty,  c.CHR_HSNCODE HSNorSAC ,  FUN_INV_GET_PURCHASE_SERIALNUMBER_CONCATS(b.CHR_TYPE,b.CHR_ITEMID, a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO) SlNos,   b.INT_QUANTITY Qty, b.DOU_UNITPRICE UnitCost, ROUND((b.INT_QUANTITY  * b.DOU_UNITPRICE),2) BeforeTax,  b.DOU_TAX_AMOUNT TaxAmt,  b.DOU_TOTAL Total, a.DOU_AMOUNT NetValue,     ROUND(a.DOU_TOTALAMOUNT,2)  FinalValue , k.CHR_TAXNAME GST, g.CHR_STAFFNAME AccountManager , FUN_GET_BRANCH_NAME(a.INT_BRANCHID)  branchname  from inv_t_directpurchase a,inv_t_directpurchaseitem b,inv_m_item c,  inv_m_division d,inv_m_vendorsinformation e,inv_m_itemgroup f, com_m_staff g ,   inv_m_vendorsgroup i, inv_m_model j, inv_m_tax k   WHERE a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO = b.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO    AND  b.CHR_ITEMID =c.CHR_ITEMID   AND b.CHR_TYPE ='I'     AND a.INT_DIVIID= d.INT_DIVIID    AND a.INT_VENDORID = e.INT_VENDORID    AND c.INT_ITEMGROUPID = f.INT_ITEMGROUPID     AND a.CHR_REF=g.CHR_EMPID    AND e.INT_GROUPID = i.INT_GROUPID   AND j.INT_MODELID = c.INT_MODELCODE   AND b.INT_TAXID = k.INT_TAXID   AND a.CHR_CANCEL = 'N'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE >= '2024-08-01'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE <= '2024-08-30'  ORDER BY a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO   )   UNION ALL    (SELECT a.CHR_VENDORPO VendorInvoice, DATE_FORMAT(a.DAT_ORDERDATE,'%d-%b-%Y') PODate, e.CHR_VENDORNAME NameoftheVendor,   e.CHR_GSTNO  VendorGSTNo,    j.CHR_MODELCODE Brand ,   b1.CHR_NAME ProductGroup, c.CHR_PRODUCTDESC PCODEDESCRIPTION  ,    FUN_INV_GET_WARRANTY_IN_YEAR(b.INT_WARRANTY) Warranty,  c.CHR_HSNCODE HSNorSAC ,  FUN_INV_GET_PURCHASE_SERIALNUMBER_CONCATS( b.CHR_TYPE,b.CHR_ITEMID,a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO) SlNos,   b.INT_QUANTITY Qty, b.DOU_UNITPRICE UnitCost, ROUND((b.INT_QUANTITY  * b.DOU_UNITPRICE),2) BeforeTax,  b.DOU_TAX_AMOUNT TaxAmt,  b.DOU_TOTAL Total, a.DOU_AMOUNT NetValue,     ROUND(a.DOU_TOTALAMOUNT,2)  FinalValue , k.CHR_TAXNAME GST,g.CHR_STAFFNAME AccountManager  , FUN_GET_BRANCH_NAME(a.INT_BRANCHID)  branchname  from inv_t_directpurchase a,inv_t_directpurchaseitem b,inv_m_produtlist c,  inv_m_productgroup b1, inv_m_division d,inv_m_vendorsinformation e,inv_m_productgroup f, com_m_staff g ,    inv_m_vendorsgroup i, inv_m_model j, inv_m_tax k   WHERE a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO = b.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO   AND b.CHR_ITEMID =c.CHR_PRODUCTID   AND b.CHR_TYPE ='P'     AND a.INT_DIVIID= d.INT_DIVIID    AND a.INT_VENDORID = e.INT_VENDORID    AND c.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID = f.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID    AND c.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID = b1.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID    AND a.CHR_REF=g.CHR_EMPID    AND e.INT_GROUPID = i.INT_GROUPID   AND j.INT_MODELID = c.CHR_MODEL   AND b.INT_TAXID = k.INT_TAXID   AND a.CHR_CANCEL = 'N'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE >= '2024-08-01'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE <= '2024-08-30'  ORDER BY a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO   )

 (SELECT  a.CHR_VENDORPO InvNumber, DATE_FORMAT(a.DAT_ORDERDATE,'%d-%b-%Y') InvDate, e.CHR_VENDORNAME NameoftheVendor,   e.CHR_GSTNO  VendorGSTNo,   j.CHR_MODELCODE Brand ,  f.CHR_GROUPNAME ProductGroup, c.CHR_DES PCODEDESCRIPTION ,     FUN_INV_GET_WARRANTY_IN_YEAR(b.INT_WARRANTY) Warranty,  c.CHR_HSNCODE HSNorSAC ,  FUN_INV_GET_PURCHASE_SERIALNUMBER_CONCATS(b.CHR_TYPE,b.CHR_ITEMID, a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO) SlNos,   b.INT_QUANTITY Qty, b.DOU_UNITPRICE UnitCost, ROUND((b.INT_QUANTITY  * b.DOU_UNITPRICE),2) BeforeTax,  b.DOU_TAX_AMOUNT TaxAmt,  b.DOU_TOTAL Total, a.DOU_AMOUNT NetValue,     ROUND(a.DOU_TOTALAMOUNT,2)  FinalValue , k.CHR_TAXNAME GST, g.CHR_STAFFNAME AccountManager , FUN_GET_BRANCH_NAME(a.INT_BRANCHID)  branchname  from inv_t_directpurchase a,inv_t_directpurchaseitem b,inv_m_item c,  inv_m_division d,inv_m_vendorsinformation e,inv_m_itemgroup f, com_m_staff g ,   inv_m_vendorsgroup i, inv_m_model j, inv_m_tax k   WHERE a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO = b.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO    AND  b.CHR_ITEMID =c.CHR_ITEMID   AND b.CHR_TYPE ='I'     AND a.INT_DIVIID= d.INT_DIVIID    AND a.INT_VENDORID = e.INT_VENDORID    AND c.INT_ITEMGROUPID = f.INT_ITEMGROUPID     AND a.CHR_REF=g.CHR_EMPID    AND e.INT_GROUPID = i.INT_GROUPID   AND j.INT_MODELID = c.INT_MODELCODE   AND b.INT_TAXID = k.INT_TAXID   AND a.CHR_CANCEL = 'N'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE >= '2024-08-01'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE <= '2024-08-30'  ORDER BY a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO   )   UNION ALL    (SELECT a.CHR_VENDORPO VendorInvoice, DATE_FORMAT(a.DAT_ORDERDATE,'%d-%b-%Y') PODate, e.CHR_VENDORNAME NameoftheVendor,   e.CHR_GSTNO  VendorGSTNo,    j.CHR_MODELCODE Brand ,   b1.CHR_NAME ProductGroup, c.CHR_PRODUCTDESC PCODEDESCRIPTION  ,    FUN_INV_GET_WARRANTY_IN_YEAR(b.INT_WARRANTY) Warranty,  c.CHR_HSNCODE HSNorSAC ,  FUN_INV_GET_PURCHASE_SERIALNUMBER_CONCATS( b.CHR_TYPE,b.CHR_ITEMID,a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO) SlNos,   b.INT_QUANTITY Qty, b.DOU_UNITPRICE UnitCost, ROUND((b.INT_QUANTITY  * b.DOU_UNITPRICE),2) BeforeTax,  b.DOU_TAX_AMOUNT TaxAmt,  b.DOU_TOTAL Total, a.DOU_AMOUNT NetValue,     ROUND(a.DOU_TOTALAMOUNT,2)  FinalValue , k.CHR_TAXNAME GST,g.CHR_STAFFNAME AccountManager  , FUN_GET_BRANCH_NAME(a.INT_BRANCHID)  branchname  from inv_t_directpurchase a,inv_t_directpurchaseitem b,inv_m_produtlist c,  inv_m_productgroup b1, inv_m_division d,inv_m_vendorsinformation e,inv_m_productgroup f, com_m_staff g ,    inv_m_vendorsgroup i, inv_m_model j, inv_m_tax k   WHERE a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO = b.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO   AND b.CHR_ITEMID =c.CHR_PRODUCTID   AND b.CHR_TYPE ='P'     AND a.INT_DIVIID= d.INT_DIVIID    AND a.INT_VENDORID = e.INT_VENDORID    AND c.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID = f.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID    AND c.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID = b1.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID    AND a.CHR_REF=g.CHR_EMPID    AND e.INT_GROUPID = i.INT_GROUPID   AND j.INT_MODELID = c.CHR_MODEL   AND b.INT_TAXID = k.INT_TAXID   AND a.CHR_CANCEL = 'N'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE >= '2024-08-01'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE <= '2024-08-30'  ORDER BY a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO   )

CHR_CITYNAME CONSIGNEE_CITY , f.CHR_DISTRICT CONSIGNEE_DISTRICT,  e.CHR_STATENAME CONSIGNEE_STATENAME,    b.INT_PINCODE CONSIGNEE_PINCODE ,IF(a.CHR_GST_TYPE='S','STATE','CENTRAL') GSTTYPE,    IF(a.CHR_SHIPPING ='D', 'Different', 'Same') CONSIGNEEADD   FROM  inv_t_directsales a, inv_m_customerinfo b, inv_t_swapsalesitem c  ,inv_m_tax d , com_m_state  e , com_m_district f, com_m_city g     WHERE  a.INT_CUSTOMERID = b.INT_CUSTOMERID     AND a.CHR_SALESNO =c.CHR_SALESNO    AND c.INT_TAXID = d.INT_TAXID     AND b.INT_STATEID = e.INT_STATEID   AND b.INT_DISTRICTID = f.INT_DISTRICTID   AND b.INT_CITYID = g.INT_CITYID   AND a.CHR_CANCEL = 'N'    AND a.INT_SALESSTATUS=1     AND a.INT_BRANCHID = 3 AND a.DAT_SALESDATE >= '2024-08-30'  AND a.DAT_SALESDATE <= '2024-08-30'  ORDER BY a.INT_SALESID
